\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doMopAction()} \\
{ Memilih aksi mop terbaik: swing bila banyak robot musuh, jika tidak mop target tunggal terbaik. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestSwing : Direction \\
\hspace*{1em} bestTarget : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} info : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} loc : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestSwing $\leftarrow$ findBestSwing() \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestSwing $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canAttack(G.me.add(bestSwing)) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.attack(G.me.add(bestSwing)) \\
\hspace*{3em} return \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} bestTarget $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ 0 \\

\hspace*{1em} for each info in G.nearbyMapInfos do \\
\hspace*{2em} loc $\leftarrow$ info.getMapLocation() \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(loc, 2) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.rc.canAttack(loc) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ 0 \\
\hspace*{2em} paint $\leftarrow$ info.getPaint() \\

\hspace*{2em} if paint.isEnemy() then \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 20 \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyEnemies) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.nearbyEnemies[i].type.isRobotType() and G.nearbyEnemies[i].location = loc then \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 10 \\
\hspace*{4em} break \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} bestTarget $\leftarrow$ loc \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestTarget $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.rc.attack(bestTarget) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findBestSwing() $\rightarrow$ Direction} \\
{ Memilih cardinal direction yang mengenai robot musuh terbanyak. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDir : Direction \\
\hspace*{1em} bestHits : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} d : Direction \\
\hspace*{1em} step1 : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} step2 : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} hits : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} enemy : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDir $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestHits $\leftarrow$ 1 \\

\hspace*{1em} for each d in \{NORTH, SOUTH, EAST, WEST\} do \\
\hspace*{2em} step1 $\leftarrow$ G.me.add(d) \\
\hspace*{2em} step2 $\leftarrow$ step1.add(d) \\
\hspace*{2em} hits $\leftarrow$ 0 \\

\hspace*{2em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyEnemies) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em} enemy $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyEnemies[i] \\
\hspace*{3em} if not enemy.type.isRobotType() then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{3em} if enemy.location.isWithinDistanceSquared(step1, 1) or enemy.location.isWithinDistanceSquared(step2, 1) then \\
\hspace*{4em} hits $\leftarrow$ hits + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\

\hspace*{2em} if hits $>$ bestHits then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestHits $\leftarrow$ hits \\
\hspace*{3em} bestDir $\leftarrow$ d \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return bestDir
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findNeedyAlly() $\rightarrow$ RobotInfo} \\
{ Mencari ally robot di sekitar yang paint-nya rendah. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ally : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} paintCap : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyAllies) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ally $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyAllies[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if not ally.type.isRobotType() then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(ally.location, 2) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} paintCap $\leftarrow$ ally.type.paintCapacity \\
\hspace*{2em} if paintCap $>$ 0 and ally.paintAmount $<$ paintCap * 0.3 then \\
\hspace*{3em} return ally \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return null
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doSupport()} \\
{ Bergerak ke ally yang butuh paint lalu transfer paint. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} ally : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} give : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} ally $\leftarrow$ findNeedyAlly() \\

\hspace*{1em} if ally = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(ally.location) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isActionReady() and G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(ally.location, 2) then \\
\hspace*{2em} give $\leftarrow$ min(G.rc.getPaint() - 10, ally.type.paintCapacity - ally.paintAmount) \\
\hspace*{2em} if give $>$ 0 and G.rc.canTransferPaint(ally.location, give) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.transferPaint(ally.location, give) \\
\hspace*{3em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doRetreat()} \\
{ Mundur ke paint tower terdekat untuk refill. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ally : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} need : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} tower : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyAllies) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ally $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyAllies[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if ally.type.isRobotType() then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if ally.type.getBaseType() $\neq$ LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(ally.location, 2) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} need $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity - G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{2em} if need $>$ 0 and G.rc.canTransferPaint(ally.location, -need) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.transferPaint(ally.location, -need) \\
\hspace*{3em} return \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} tower $\leftarrow$ findNearestPaintTowerLoc() \\
\hspace*{1em} if tower $\neq$ null and G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(tower) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure tryCompletePatterns()} \\
{ Menyelesaikan tower pattern yang sudah siap di ruin sekitar. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ruin : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} tt : UnitType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyRuins) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ruin $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyRuins[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} for each tt in Soldier.TOWER\_TYPES do \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.canCompleteTowerPattern(tt, ruin) then \\
\hspace*{4em} G.rc.completeTowerPattern(tt, ruin) \\
\hspace*{4em} call MapMemory.registerRuin(ruin, G.team, tt) \\
\hspace*{4em} break \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findNearestPaintTowerLoc() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Mengembalikan lokasi ally paint tower terdekat. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} d, i : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist $\leftarrow +\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.numRuins - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} if MapMemory.ruinTeams[i] = G.team and MapMemory.ruinTypes[i] $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em} if MapMemory.ruinTypes[i].getBaseType() = LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER then \\
\hspace*{4em} d $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(MapMemory.ruinLocs[i]) \\
\hspace*{4em} if d $<$ bestDist then \\
\hspace*{5em} bestDist $\leftarrow$ d \\
\hspace*{5em} best $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.ruinLocs[i] \\
\hspace*{4em} endif \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return best
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function modeStr() $\rightarrow$ string} \\
{ Mengubah kode mode Mopper menjadi string. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} switch mode \\
\hspace*{2em} case EXPLORE : return "EXP" \\
\hspace*{2em} case SUPPORT : return "SUP" \\
\hspace*{2em} case RETREAT : return "RET" \\
\hspace*{2em} default      : return "?" \\
\hspace*{1em} endswitch
\end{pseudobox}



\item \textbf{Soldier.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure init()} \\
{ Inisialisasi pattern tower dan resource serta seed random. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} towerPatterns $\leftarrow$ array berisi pattern paint, money, defense tower \\
\hspace*{1em} resourcePattern $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getResourcePattern() \\
\hspace*{1em} Rand.state $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getID() * 1234567 + 89
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure run()} \\
{ Prosedur utama Soldier tiap round. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} paintCap : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} call opportunisticComplete() \\
\hspace*{1em} call tryCompleteResourcePatterns() \\
\hspace*{1em} call tryWithdrawPaint() \\

\hspace*{1em} paint $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{1em} paintCap $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity \\

\hspace*{1em} if paint $<$ 35 and mode $\neq$ RETREAT then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ RETREAT \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if mode = RETREAT and paint $\geq$ 0.72 * paintCap then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round $>$ TOWER\_BUILD\_CUTOFF and mode $\neq$ RETREAT and mode $\neq$ ATTACK then \\
\hspace*{2em} if mode $\neq$ EXPLORE then \\
\hspace*{3em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if mode $\neq$ RETREAT then \\
\hspace*{2em} switch mode \\
\hspace*{3em} case EXPLORE   : call transitionExplore() \\
\hspace*{3em} case GOTO\_RUIN : call transitionGotoRuin() \\
\hspace*{3em} case MARK      : call transitionMark() \\
\hspace*{3em} case PAINT     : call transitionPaint() \\
\hspace*{3em} case COMPLETE  : call transitionComplete() \\
\hspace*{3em} case ATTACK    : call transitionAttack() \\
\hspace*{2em} endswitch \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} switch mode \\
\hspace*{2em} case EXPLORE   : call doExplore() \\
\hspace*{2em} case GOTO\_RUIN : call doGotoRuin() \\
\hspace*{2em} case MARK      : call doMark() \\
\hspace*{2em} case PAINT     : call doPaint() \\
\hspace*{2em} case COMPLETE  : call doComplete() \\
\hspace*{2em} case RETREAT   : call doRetreat() \\
\hspace*{2em} case ATTACK    : call doAttack() \\
\hspace*{1em} endswitch \\

\hspace*{1em} append indicator
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure transitionExplore()} \\
{ Dari explore, tentukan apakah harus menyerang atau mulai membangun tower. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} ruin : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.round $>$ TOWER\_BUILD\_CUTOFF then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.lastKnownEnemyTower $\neq$ null and G.round - G.lastEnemyTowerRound $<$ 60 and G.rc.getPaint() $>$ 130 then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ ATTACK \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} ruin $\leftarrow$ findBestRuinToBuild() \\

\hspace*{1em} if ruin $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} targetRuin $\leftarrow$ ruin \\
\hspace*{2em} targetType $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.chooseTowerType() \\
\hspace*{2em} markerPlaced $\leftarrow$ false \\
\hspace*{2em} buildStartRound $\leftarrow$ G.round \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ GOTO\_RUIN \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure transitionGotoRuin()} \\
{ Periksa apakah target ruin masih valid dan apakah sudah cukup dekat untuk mark. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round - buildStartRound $>$ BUILD\_TIMEOUT then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call checkRuinStolen() \\

\hspace*{1em} if mode $\neq$ GOTO\_RUIN then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.canSenseLocation(targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ MARK \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure transitionMark()} \\
{ Periksa apakah fase marking bisa lanjut. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round - buildStartRound $>$ BUILD\_TIMEOUT then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call checkRuinStolen() \\

\hspace*{1em} if mode $\neq$ MARK then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if markerPlaced then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ PAINT \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure transitionPaint()} \\
{ Saat fase paint, cek apakah pattern sudah lengkap dan siap complete. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} k : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round - buildStartRound $>$ BUILD\_TIMEOUT then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call checkRuinStolen() \\

\hspace*{1em} if mode $\neq$ PAINT then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(targetRuin, 20) then \\
\hspace*{2em} for k $\leftarrow$ 0 to length(TOWER\_TYPES) - 1 do \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.canCompleteTowerPattern(TOWER\_TYPES[k], targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{4em} targetType $\leftarrow$ TOWER\_TYPES[k] \\
\hspace*{4em} mode $\leftarrow$ COMPLETE \\
\hspace*{4em} return \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure transitionComplete()} \\
{ Coba complete tower; jika gagal kembali ke fase paint. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not tryCompleteTower() then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ PAINT \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure transitionAttack()} \\
{ Keluar dari attack jika info enemy tower sudah usang. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.lastKnownEnemyTower = null or G.round - G.lastEnemyTowerRound $>$ 80 then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doExplore()} \\
{ Mode eksplorasi umum Soldier. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if exploreTarget = null or G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(exploreTarget, 4) or exploreAge $>$ EXPLORE\_TIMEOUT then \\
\hspace*{2em} exploreTarget $\leftarrow$ pickGreedyExploreTarget() \\
\hspace*{2em} exploreAge $\leftarrow$ 0 \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} exploreAge $\leftarrow$ exploreAge + 1 \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(exploreTarget) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call paintNearby()
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doGotoRuin()} \\
{ Bergerak menuju ruin target sambil tetap mengecat sekitar. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call paintNearby()
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doMark()} \\
{ Menempatkan marker tower pattern di ruin target. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not G.rc.canSenseLocation(targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{3em} call Nav.moveToward(targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} call paintNearby() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not markerPlaced then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.getPaint() $<$ 30 then \\
\hspace*{3em} call paintNearby() \\
\hspace*{3em} return \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canMarkTowerPattern(targetType, targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.markTowerPattern(targetType, targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{3em} markerPlaced $\leftarrow$ true \\
\hspace*{3em} mode $\leftarrow$ PAINT \\
\hspace*{3em} return \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() and not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(targetRuin, 4) then \\
\hspace*{3em} call Nav.moveToward(targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} mode $\leftarrow$ PAINT
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doPaint()} \\
{ Mengecat pattern tower seefisien mungkin. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} urgent : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} urgent $\leftarrow$ findUrgentPatternTile() \\
\hspace*{2em} if urgent $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(urgent, G.rc.getType().actionRadiusSquared) then \\
\hspace*{4em} call Nav.moveToward(urgent) \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} else \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(targetRuin, 4) then \\
\hspace*{4em} call Nav.moveToward(targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} if not paintBestPatternTile() then \\
\hspace*{3em} call paintNearby() \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doComplete()} \\
{ Mendekat ke ruin lalu complete tower. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(targetRuin, 2) then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{3em} call Nav.moveToward(targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call tryCompleteTower()
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doRetreat()} \\
{ Mundur ke ally tower terdekat untuk refill paint. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} tower : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} need : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} tower $\leftarrow$ findNearestAllyTowerLoc() \\

\hspace*{1em} if tower = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{3em} call Nav.moveToward(G.mapCenter) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(tower) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(tower, 2) then \\
\hspace*{2em} need $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity - G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{2em} if need $>$ 0 and G.rc.canTransferPaint(tower, -need) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.transferPaint(tower, -need) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doAttack()} \\
{ Bergerak menuju enemy tower terakhir yang diketahui dan menyerangnya. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} target : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} target $\leftarrow$ G.lastKnownEnemyTower \\

\hspace*{1em} if target = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(target) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(target, G.rc.getType().actionRadiusSquared) then \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.canAttack(target) then \\
\hspace*{4em} G.rc.attack(target) \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} else \\
\hspace*{3em} call paintNearby() \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function paintBestPatternTile() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
{ Memilih tile pattern paling prioritas untuk dicat. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestTile : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestUseSecondary : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} bestPriority : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} actionRad : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} mopperNearby : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} dx, dy : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} tile : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} info : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} mark : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} needSecondary : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} priority : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} dist : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return false \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} bestTile $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestUseSecondary $\leftarrow$ false \\
\hspace*{1em} bestPriority $\leftarrow$ -1 \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist $\leftarrow +\infty$ \\
\hspace*{1em} actionRad $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().actionRadiusSquared \\
\hspace*{1em} mopperNearby $\leftarrow$ hasMopperNearby() \\

\hspace*{1em} for dy $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{2em} for dx $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{3em} tile $\leftarrow$ targetRuin.translate(dx, dy) \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(tile, actionRad) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.rc.canSenseLocation(tile) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.rc.canAttack(tile) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} info $\leftarrow$ G.rc.senseMapInfo(tile) \\
\hspace*{3em} mark $\leftarrow$ info.getMark() \\
\hspace*{3em} paint $\leftarrow$ info.getPaint() \\

\hspace*{3em} if mark = EMPTY then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} needSecondary $\leftarrow$ (mark = ALLY\_SECONDARY) \\

\hspace*{3em} if needSecondary and paint = ALLY\_SECONDARY then continue endif \\
\hspace*{3em} if not needSecondary and paint = ALLY\_PRIMARY then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} if paint.isEnemy() then \\
\hspace*{4em} if mopperNearby and G.round - buildStartRound $<$ 40 then \\
\hspace*{5em} priority $\leftarrow$ 1 \\
\hspace*{4em} else \\
\hspace*{5em} priority $\leftarrow$ 2 \\
\hspace*{4em} endif \\
\hspace*{3em} else \\
\hspace*{4em} priority $\leftarrow$ 3 \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\

\hspace*{3em} dist $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(tile) \\

\hspace*{3em} if priority $>$ bestPriority or (priority = bestPriority and dist $<$ bestDist) then \\
\hspace*{4em} bestPriority $\leftarrow$ priority \\
\hspace*{4em} bestDist $\leftarrow$ dist \\
\hspace*{4em} bestTile $\leftarrow$ tile \\
\hspace*{4em} bestUseSecondary $\leftarrow$ needSecondary \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestTile = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return false \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestUseSecondary then \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canAttack(bestTile) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.attack(bestTile, true) \\
\hspace*{3em} return true \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} else \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canAttack(bestTile) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.attack(bestTile) \\
\hspace*{3em} return true \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findUrgentPatternTile() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Mengembalikan tile pattern terdekat yang masih perlu dicat. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} mopperNearby : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} dx, dy : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} tile : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} info : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} mark : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} needSecondary : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} d : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return null \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist $\leftarrow +\infty$ \\
\hspace*{1em} mopperNearby $\leftarrow$ hasMopperNearby() \\

\hspace*{1em} for dy $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{2em} for dx $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{3em} tile $\leftarrow$ targetRuin.translate(dx, dy) \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.rc.canSenseLocation(tile) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} info $\leftarrow$ G.rc.senseMapInfo(tile) \\
\hspace*{3em} mark $\leftarrow$ info.getMark() \\
\hspace*{3em} paint $\leftarrow$ info.getPaint() \\

\hspace*{3em} if mark = EMPTY then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} needSecondary $\leftarrow$ (mark = ALLY\_SECONDARY) \\
\hspace*{3em} if needSecondary and paint = ALLY\_SECONDARY then continue endif \\
\hspace*{3em} if not needSecondary and paint = ALLY\_PRIMARY then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} if paint.isEnemy() and mopperNearby and G.round - buildStartRound $<$ 40 then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\

\hspace*{3em} d $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(tile) \\
\hspace*{3em} if d $<$ bestDist then \\
\hspace*{4em} bestDist $\leftarrow$ d \\
\hspace*{4em} best $\leftarrow$ tile \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return best
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function hasMopperNearby() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
{ Mengecek apakah ada ally Mopper di sekitar. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} r : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyAllies) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} r $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyAllies[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if r.type = MOPPER and G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(r.location, 8) then \\
\hspace*{3em} return true \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure opportunisticComplete()} \\
{ Menyelesaikan pattern tower siap-complete di ruin sekitar meskipun bukan target utama. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i, k : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ruin : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} ri : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} sentinel : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyRuins) - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ruin $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyRuins[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canSenseRobotAtLocation(ruin) then \\
\hspace*{3em} ri $\leftarrow$ G.rc.senseRobotAtLocation(ruin) \\
\hspace*{3em} if ri $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} for k $\leftarrow$ 0 to length(TOWER\_TYPES) - 1 do \\
\hspace*{3em} if not G.rc.canCompleteTowerPattern(TOWER\_TYPES[k], ruin) then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\

\hspace*{3em} sentinel $\leftarrow$ ruin.add(SENTINEL[k]) \\

\hspace*{3em} if not G.rc.canSenseLocation(sentinel) then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\

\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.senseMapInfo(sentinel).getMark() = EMPTY then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\

\hspace*{3em} G.rc.completeTowerPattern(TOWER\_TYPES[k], ruin) \\
\hspace*{3em} call MapMemory.registerRuin(ruin, G.team, TOWER\_TYPES[k]) \\

\hspace*{3em} if ruin = targetRuin then \\
\hspace*{4em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{3em} break \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function tryCompleteTower() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
{ Mencoba complete tower pada targetRuin. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} k : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} sentinel : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return false \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} for k $\leftarrow$ 0 to length(TOWER\_TYPES) - 1 do \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.rc.canCompleteTowerPattern(TOWER\_TYPES[k], targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{3em} continue \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} sentinel $\leftarrow$ targetRuin.add(SENTINEL[k]) \\

\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canSenseLocation(sentinel) then \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.senseMapInfo(sentinel).getMark() = EMPTY then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} G.rc.completeTowerPattern(TOWER\_TYPES[k], targetRuin) \\
\hspace*{2em} call MapMemory.registerRuin(targetRuin, G.team, TOWER\_TYPES[k]) \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} return true \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findBestRuinToBuild() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Memilih ruin terbaik secara greedy untuk dibangun. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ruin : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} ri : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} enemyPaint : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ -\infty \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyRuins)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ruin $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyRuins[i] \\

\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canSenseRobotAtLocation(ruin) then \\
\hspace*{3em} ri $\leftarrow$ G.rc.senseRobotAtLocation(ruin) \\
\hspace*{3em} if ri $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4em} continue \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} enemyPaint $\leftarrow$ countEnemyPaint(ruin, 12) \\
\hspace*{2em} if enemyPaint $>$ 12 then \\
\hspace*{3em} continue \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ 1500 - G.me.distanceSquaredTo(ruin) - enemyPaint * 12 \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} best $\leftarrow$ ruin \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.numRuins - 1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} if MapMemory.ruinStatus[i] $\neq$ 0 then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.round - MapMemory.ruinClaimRound[i] $<$ 100 then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ 1000 - G.me.distanceSquaredTo(MapMemory.ruinLocs[i]) \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} best $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.ruinLocs[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return best
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function countEnemyPaint(loc : MapLocation, radiusSq : integer) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
{ Menghitung jumlah enemy paint di sekitar loc. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} count : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} area : array of MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} j : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} count $\leftarrow$ 0 \\
\hspace*{1em} area $\leftarrow$ G.rc.senseNearbyMapInfos(loc, radiusSq) \\

\hspace*{1em} for j $\leftarrow$ length(area)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} if area[j].getPaint().isEnemy() then \\
\hspace*{3em} count $\leftarrow$ count + 1 \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return count
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function pickGreedyExploreTarget() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Memilih target eksplorasi terbaik berdasarkan ruin netral dan random sampling. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} i, t : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ruin, c : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} v, dist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ -1 \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round $\leq$ TOWER\_BUILD\_CUTOFF then \\
\hspace*{2em} for i $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.numRuins-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em} if MapMemory.ruinStatus[i] $\neq$ 0 then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} ruin $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.ruinLocs[i] \\
\hspace*{3em} v $\leftarrow$ G.getVisited(ruin) \\
\hspace*{3em} dist $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(ruin) \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ 250 - (if v = 0 then 0 else min(80, G.round - v)) - dist div 6 \\

\hspace*{3em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{4em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{4em} best $\leftarrow$ ruin \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} for t $\leftarrow$ 0 to 7 do \\
\hspace*{2em} c $\leftarrow$ MapLocation(Rand.nextInt(G.mapWidth), Rand.nextInt(G.mapHeight)) \\
\hspace*{2em} v $\leftarrow$ G.getVisited(c) \\
\hspace*{2em} dist $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(c) \\
\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ (if v = 0 then 160 else max(0, G.round - v - 30)) + dist div 8 \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} best $\leftarrow$ c \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if best $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return best \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return MapLocation(Rand.nextInt(G.mapWidth), Rand.nextInt(G.mapHeight))
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure paintNearby()} \\
{ Memilih tile sekitar terbaik untuk dicat demi ekspansi territory. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} info : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} loc : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if not G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ 0 \\

\hspace*{1em} for each info in G.nearbyMapInfos do \\
\hspace*{2em} loc $\leftarrow$ info.getMapLocation() \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(loc, G.rc.getType().actionRadiusSquared) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.rc.canAttack(loc) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if info.isWall() or info.hasRuin() then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} paint $\leftarrow$ info.getPaint() \\

\hspace*{2em} if paint = EMPTY then \\
\hspace*{3em} if loc = G.me then \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ 120 \\
\hspace*{3em} else \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ 100 \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} else if paint.isEnemy() then \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ 80 \\
\hspace*{2em} else \\
\hspace*{3em} continue \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ score - G.me.distanceSquaredTo(loc) \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} best $\leftarrow$ loc \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if best $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.rc.attack(best) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure tryCompleteResourcePatterns()} \\
{ Menyelesaikan resource pattern di sekitar posisi Soldier. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} dx, dy : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} loc : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for dx $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{2em} for dy $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{3em} loc $\leftarrow$ G.me.translate(dx, dy) \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.canCompleteResourcePattern(loc) then \\
\hspace*{4em} G.rc.completeResourcePattern(loc) \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure tryWithdrawPaint()} \\
{ Mengambil paint dari nearby ally paint tower jika paint belum hampir penuh. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ally : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} need : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.getPaint() $\geq$ 0.88 * G.rc.getType().paintCapacity then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyAllies)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ally $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyAllies[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if ally.type.isRobotType() then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if ally.type.getBaseType() $\neq$ LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(ally.location, 2) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} need $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity - G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{2em} if need $>$ 0 and G.rc.canTransferPaint(ally.location, -need) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.transferPaint(ally.location, -need) \\
\hspace*{3em} return \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findNearestAllyTowerLoc() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Mengembalikan ally tower terdekat, dengan prioritas lebih tinggi untuk paint tower. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} isPaint : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} d, i : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist $\leftarrow +\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.numRuins-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} if MapMemory.ruinTeams[i] $\neq$ G.team or MapMemory.ruinTypes[i] = null then \\
\hspace*{3em} continue \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} isPaint $\leftarrow$ (MapMemory.ruinTypes[i].getBaseType() = LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER) \\
\hspace*{2em} d $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(MapMemory.ruinLocs[i]) + (if isPaint then 0 else 400) \\

\hspace*{2em} if d $<$ bestDist then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestDist $\leftarrow$ d \\
\hspace*{3em} best $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.ruinLocs[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return best
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure checkRuinStolen()} \\
{ Mengecek apakah target ruin sudah diambil musuh atau ally. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} ri : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if targetRuin = null or not G.rc.canSenseLocation(targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not G.rc.canSenseRobotAtLocation(targetRuin) then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} ri $\leftarrow$ G.rc.senseRobotAtLocation(targetRuin) \\

\hspace*{1em} if ri = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if ri.team = G.opponentTeam then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.lastKnownEnemyTower $\leftarrow$ targetRuin \\
\hspace*{2em} G.lastEnemyTowerRound $\leftarrow$ G.round \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ ATTACK \\
\hspace*{1em} else if ri.team = G.team then \\
\hspace*{2em} call MapMemory.registerRuin(targetRuin, G.team, ri.type) \\
\hspace*{2em} call abandonBuild() \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure abandonBuild()} \\
{ Menghapus state pembangunan tower dan kembali ke EXPLORE. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} targetRuin $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} targetType $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} markerPlaced $\leftarrow$ false \\
\hspace*{1em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function modeStr() $\rightarrow$ string} \\
{ Mengubah mode Soldier menjadi string singkat. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} switch mode \\
\hspace*{2em} case EXPLORE   : return "EXP" \\
\hspace*{2em} case GOTO\_RUIN : return "GTO" \\
\hspace*{2em} case MARK      : return "MRK" \\
\hspace*{2em} case PAINT     : return "PNT" \\
\hspace*{2em} case COMPLETE  : return "CMP" \\
\hspace*{2em} case RETREAT   : return "RET" \\
\hspace*{2em} case ATTACK    : return "ATK" \\
\hspace*{2em} default        : return "?" \\
\hspace*{1em} endswitch
\end{pseudobox}



\item \textbf{Splasher.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure init()} \\
{ Inisialisasi seed random Splasher. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} Rand.state $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getID() * 7654321 + 13
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure run()} \\
{ Prosedur utama Splasher tiap round. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} cap : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} call tryWithdrawPaint() \\

\hspace*{1em} paint $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{1em} cap $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity \\

\hspace*{1em} if paint $<$ 50 and mode $\neq$ RETREAT then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ RETREAT \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if mode = RETREAT and paint $\geq$ 0.75 * cap then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call tryCompletePatterns() \\

\hspace*{1em} switch mode \\
\hspace*{2em} case EXPLORE : call doExplore() \\
\hspace*{2em} case RETREAT : call doRetreat() \\
\hspace*{1em} endswitch \\

\hspace*{1em} append indicator
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doExplore()} \\
{ Explore lalu lakukan splash attack terbaik. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} moveTarget : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} moveTarget $\leftarrow$ pickMoveTarget() \\
\hspace*{2em} if moveTarget $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em} call Nav.moveToward(moveTarget) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call bestSplash() \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure bestSplash()} \\
{ Memilih pusat splash dengan skor area terbaik. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestLoc : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} actionRadius : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} info, tile : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} center : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} paint : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1em} tileLoc : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestLoc $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ 0 \\
\hspace*{1em} actionRadius $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().actionRadiusSquared \\

\hspace*{1em} for each info in G.nearbyMapInfos do \\
\hspace*{2em} center $\leftarrow$ info.getMapLocation() \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(center, actionRadius) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.rc.canAttack(center) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ 0 \\

\hspace*{2em} for each tile in G.nearbyMapInfos do \\
\hspace*{3em} tileLoc $\leftarrow$ tile.getMapLocation() \\
\hspace*{3em} if not center.isWithinDistanceSquared(tileLoc, 2) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{3em} paint $\leftarrow$ tile.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{3em} if paint = EMPTY then \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 10 \\
\hspace*{3em} else if paint.isEnemy() then \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 15 \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\

\hspace*{2em} if G.getVisited(center) = 0 then \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 12 \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} for each tile in G.nearbyMapInfos do \\
\hspace*{3em} tileLoc $\leftarrow$ tile.getMapLocation() \\
\hspace*{3em} if not center.isWithinDistanceSquared(tileLoc, 2) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.getVisited(tileLoc) = 0 then \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 3 \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} bestLoc $\leftarrow$ center \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestLoc $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.rc.attack(bestLoc) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function pickMoveTarget() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Memilih target gerak berdasarkan target explore dan scoring local next step. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDir : Direction \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} d : Direction \\
\hspace*{1em} next : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} v : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if exploreTarget = null or G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(exploreTarget, 4) or exploreAge $>$ EXPLORE\_TIMEOUT then \\
\hspace*{2em} exploreTarget $\leftarrow$ pickGreedyTarget() \\
\hspace*{2em} exploreAge $\leftarrow$ 0 \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} exploreAge $\leftarrow$ exploreAge + 1 \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDir $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ -\infty \\

\hspace*{1em} for each d in G.DIRECTIONS do \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.rc.canMove(d) then \\
\hspace*{3em} continue \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} next $\leftarrow$ G.me.add(d) \\
\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ 0 \\

\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ score + (G.me.distanceSquaredTo(exploreTarget) - next.distanceSquaredTo(exploreTarget)) * 5 \\

\hspace*{2em} v $\leftarrow$ G.getVisited(next) \\
\hspace*{2em} if v = 0 then \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ score + 30 \\
\hspace*{2em} else \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ score - (G.round - v) div 5 \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyAllies)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.nearbyAllies[i].type.isRobotType() and next.isWithinDistanceSquared(G.nearbyAllies[i].location, 2) then \\
\hspace*{4em} score $\leftarrow$ score - 20 \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} bestDir $\leftarrow$ d \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestDir $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return G.me.add(bestDir) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return exploreTarget
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function pickGreedyTarget() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Memilih target eksplorasi acak yang paling menjanjikan. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} attempt : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} x, y : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} c : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} v, dist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ -1 \\

\hspace*{1em} for attempt $\leftarrow$ 0 to 7 do \\
\hspace*{2em} x $\leftarrow$ Rand.nextInt(G.mapWidth) \\
\hspace*{2em} y $\leftarrow$ Rand.nextInt(G.mapHeight) \\
\hspace*{2em} c $\leftarrow$ MapLocation(x, y) \\

\hspace*{2em} v $\leftarrow$ G.getVisited(c) \\
\hspace*{2em} dist $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(c) \\
\hspace*{2em} score $\leftarrow$ (if v = 0 then 100 else max(0, G.round - v - 20)) + dist div 10 \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} best $\leftarrow$ c \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if best $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{2em} return best \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return MapLocation(Rand.nextInt(G.mapWidth), Rand.nextInt(G.mapHeight))
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doRetreat()} \\
{ Mundur ke paint tower terdekat. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} tower : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} need : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} tower $\leftarrow$ findNearestPaintTowerLoc() \\

\hspace*{1em} if tower = null then \\
\hspace*{2em} mode $\leftarrow$ EXPLORE \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.isMovementReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} call Nav.moveToward(tower) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(tower, 2) then \\
\hspace*{2em} need $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity - G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{2em} if need $>$ 0 and G.rc.canTransferPaint(tower, -need) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.transferPaint(tower, -need) \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure tryWithdrawPaint()} \\
{ Mengambil paint dari nearby ally paint tower jika belum hampir penuh. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ally : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} need : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.getPaint() $\geq$ 0.9 * G.rc.getType().paintCapacity then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if not G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} return \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyAllies)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ally $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyAllies[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if ally.type.isRobotType() then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if ally.type.getBaseType() $\neq$ LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(ally.location, 2) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} need $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().paintCapacity - G.rc.getPaint() \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canTransferPaint(ally.location, -need) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.transferPaint(ally.location, -need) \\
\hspace*{3em} return \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure tryCompletePatterns()} \\
{ Menyelesaikan tower pattern dan resource pattern yang siap. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} i, dx, dy : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} ruin : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} tt : UnitType \\
\hspace*{1em} loc : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyRuins)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} ruin $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyRuins[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} for each tt in Soldier.TOWER\_TYPES do \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.canCompleteTowerPattern(tt, ruin) then \\
\hspace*{4em} G.rc.completeTowerPattern(tt, ruin) \\
\hspace*{4em} call MapMemory.registerRuin(ruin, G.team, tt) \\
\hspace*{4em} break \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} for dx $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{2em} for dy $\leftarrow$ -2 to 2 do \\
\hspace*{3em} loc $\leftarrow$ G.me.translate(dx, dy) \\
\hspace*{3em} if G.rc.canCompleteResourcePattern(loc) then \\
\hspace*{4em} G.rc.completeResourcePattern(loc) \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endfor \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function findNearestPaintTowerLoc() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
{ Mengembalikan ally paint tower terdekat. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} best : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} d, i : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} best $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestDist $\leftarrow +\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.numRuins-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} if MapMemory.ruinTeams[i] = G.team and MapMemory.ruinTypes[i] $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em} if MapMemory.ruinTypes[i].getBaseType() = LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER then \\
\hspace*{4em} d $\leftarrow$ G.me.distanceSquaredTo(MapMemory.ruinLocs[i]) \\
\hspace*{4em} if d $<$ bestDist then \\
\hspace*{5em} bestDist $\leftarrow$ d \\
\hspace*{5em} best $\leftarrow$ MapMemory.ruinLocs[i] \\
\hspace*{4em} endif \\
\hspace*{3em} endif \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return best
\end{pseudobox}



\item \textbf{Tower.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure init()} \\
{ Inisialisasi daftar lokasi spawn dan mendaftarkan tower ini ke MapMemory. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} spawnLocs $\leftarrow$ buildSpawnLocs() \\
\hspace*{1em} call MapMemory.registerRuin(G.me, G.team, G.rc.getType())
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure run()} \\
{ Logic utama Tower setiap round. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} isPhase2 : boolean \\
\hspace*{1em} toSpawn : UnitType \\
\hspace*{1em} shouldSpawnNow : boolean

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} call MapMemory.scanNearby() \\

\hspace*{1em} isPhase2 $\leftarrow$ (G.round $\geq$ PHASE2\_ROUND) \\

\hspace*{1em} if isPhase2 then \\
\hspace*{2em} toSpawn $\leftarrow$ decideSpawnPhase2() \\
\hspace*{1em} else \\
\hspace*{2em} toSpawn $\leftarrow$ decideSpawnPhase1() \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} shouldSpawnNow $\leftarrow$ shouldSpawn(toSpawn, isPhase2) \\

\hspace*{1em} if shouldSpawnNow and G.rc.isActionReady() then \\
\hspace*{2em} if trySpawn(toSpawn) then \\
\hspace*{3em} lastSpawnRound $\leftarrow$ G.round \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} call doAttack() \\
\hspace*{1em} call tryUpgrade() \\

\hspace*{1em} append indicator
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function decideSpawnPhase1() $\rightarrow$ UnitType} \\
{ Memilih tipe robot untuk fase awal secara greedy berdasarkan demand berbobot. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} wS, wSp, wM : real \\
\hspace*{1em} sum : real \\
\hspace*{1em} scoreS, scoreSp, scoreM : real

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.round $<$ 50 and spawnedTotal $<$ 5 then \\
\hspace*{2em} return SOLDIER \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} wS $\leftarrow$ W\_SOLDIER\_BASE \\
\hspace*{1em} wSp $\leftarrow$ W\_SPLASHER\_BASE \\
\hspace*{1em} wM $\leftarrow$ W\_MOPPER\_BASE \\

\hspace*{1em} wSp $\leftarrow$ wSp + MapMemory.allyPaintTowers * 0.35 \\
\hspace*{1em} wS $\leftarrow$ wS - min(1.0, G.rc.getNumberTowers() * 0.04) \\

\hspace*{1em} if MapMemory.allyMoneyTowers $\geq$ 2 then \\
\hspace*{2em} wSp $\leftarrow$ wSp + 0.4 \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} sum $\leftarrow$ max(0.01, wS + wSp + wM) \\
\hspace*{1em} wS $\leftarrow$ wS / sum \\
\hspace*{1em} wSp $\leftarrow$ wSp / sum \\
\hspace*{1em} wM $\leftarrow$ wM / sum \\

\hspace*{1em} scoreS $\leftarrow$ dSoldier + wS - spawnedSoldiers \\
\hspace*{1em} scoreSp $\leftarrow$ dSplasher + wSp - spawnedSplashers \\
\hspace*{1em} scoreM $\leftarrow$ dMopper + wM - spawnedMoppers \\

\hspace*{1em} if scoreS $\geq$ scoreSp and scoreS $\geq$ scoreM then \\
\hspace*{2em} return SOLDIER \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if scoreSp $\geq$ scoreM then \\
\hspace*{2em} return SPLASHER \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return MOPPER
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function decideSpawnPhase2() $\rightarrow$ UnitType} \\
{ Fase akhir: mayoritas spawn Splasher, sesekali Mopper. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} -

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if (spawnedTotal mod 7) = 6 then \\
\hspace*{2em} return MOPPER \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return SPLASHER
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function shouldSpawn(toSpawn : UnitType, isPhase2 : boolean) $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
{ Menentukan apakah tower layak spawn sekarang. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} chips : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} towers : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} cost : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} chips $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getMoney() \\
\hspace*{1em} towers $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getNumberTowers() \\
\hspace*{1em} cost $\leftarrow$ toSpawn.moneyCost \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round $<$ 10 then \\
\hspace*{2em} return true \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if towers $\geq$ 25 then \\
\hspace*{2em} return (chips $\geq$ cost) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if chips $<$ cost then \\
\hspace*{2em} return false \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if isPhase2 then \\
\hspace*{2em} return true \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if chips - cost $\geq$ 600 then return true endif \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.round $<$ 80 then return true endif \\
\hspace*{1em} if lastSpawnRound + 2 $<$ G.round and length(G.nearbyAllies) $<$ 3 then return true endif \\

\hspace*{1em} return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function trySpawn(type : UnitType) $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
{ Mencoba spawn robot pada lokasi spawn pertama yang valid. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} loc : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} for each loc in spawnLocs do \\
\hspace*{2em} if G.rc.canBuildRobot(type, loc) then \\
\hspace*{3em} G.rc.buildRobot(type, loc) \\
\hspace*{3em} spawnedTotal $\leftarrow$ spawnedTotal + 1 \\

\hspace*{3em} switch type \\
\hspace*{4em} case SOLDIER  : spawnedSoldiers $\leftarrow$ spawnedSoldiers + 1 ; dSoldier $\leftarrow$ dSoldier + 1 \\
\hspace*{4em} case SPLASHER : spawnedSplashers $\leftarrow$ spawnedSplashers + 1 ; dSplasher $\leftarrow$ dSplasher + 1 \\
\hspace*{4em} case MOPPER   : spawnedMoppers $\leftarrow$ spawnedMoppers + 1 ; dMopper $\leftarrow$ dMopper + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em} endswitch \\

\hspace*{3em} return true \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure doAttack()} \\
{ Menyerang area dengan AoE lalu memilih target tunggal terbaik. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} bestTarget : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} atkStr : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} i : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} r : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1em} score : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.canAttack(null) then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.rc.attack(null) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} bestTarget $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ -\infty \\
\hspace*{1em} atkStr $\leftarrow$ G.rc.getType().attackStrength \\

\hspace*{1em} for i $\leftarrow$ length(G.nearbyEnemies)-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{2em} r $\leftarrow$ G.nearbyEnemies[i] \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.me.isWithinDistanceSquared(r.location, G.rc.getType().actionRadiusSquared) then continue endif \\
\hspace*{2em} if not G.rc.canSenseRobotAtLocation(r.location) then continue endif \\

\hspace*{2em} if r.health $\leq$ atkStr then \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ 10000 - r.health \\
\hspace*{2em} else \\
\hspace*{3em} score $\leftarrow$ 1000 - r.health \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\

\hspace*{2em} if score $>$ bestScore then \\
\hspace*{3em} bestScore $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{3em} bestTarget $\leftarrow$ r.location \\
\hspace*{2em} endif \\
\hspace*{1em} endfor \\

\hspace*{1em} if bestTarget $\neq$ null and G.rc.canAttack(bestTarget) then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.rc.attack(bestTarget) \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure tryUpgrade()} \\
{ Upgrade tower bila uang cukup dan buffer aman. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} upgradeCost : integer \\
\hspace*{1em} buffer : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} if towerLevel = 0 then \\
\hspace*{2em} upgradeCost $\leftarrow$ 2500 \\
\hspace*{1em} else \\
\hspace*{2em} upgradeCost $\leftarrow$ 5000 \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.round $\geq$ PHASE2\_ROUND then \\
\hspace*{2em} buffer $\leftarrow$ 400 \\
\hspace*{1em} else \\
\hspace*{2em} buffer $\leftarrow$ 1000 \\
\hspace*{1em} endif \\

\hspace*{1em} if G.rc.getMoney() - upgradeCost $\geq$ buffer and G.rc.canUpgradeTower(G.me) then \\
\hspace*{2em} G.rc.upgradeTower(G.me) \\
\hspace*{2em} towerLevel $\leftarrow$ towerLevel + 1 \\
\hspace*{1em} endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function buildSpawnLocs() $\rightarrow$ array of MapLocation} \\
{ Membangun daftar lokasi spawn di sekitar tower lalu mengurutkannya relatif terhadap pusat peta. }

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1em} locs : array of MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1em} center : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1em} locs $\leftarrow$ \{ \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + NORTH, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + NORTHEAST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + EAST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + SOUTHEAST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + SOUTH, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + SOUTHWEST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + WEST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + NORTHWEST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + NORTH + NORTH, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + EAST + EAST, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + SOUTH + SOUTH, \\
\hspace*{2em} G.me + WEST + WEST \\
\hspace*{1em} \} \\

\hspace*{1em} center $\leftarrow$ G.mapCenter \\
\hspace*{1em} sort locs descending by distanceSquaredTo(center) \\

\hspace*{1em} return locs
\end{pseudobox}

\subsubsection{Algoritma Solusi Greedy 2}

\begin{enumerate}[label=\alph*.]

\item \texttt{RobotPlayer.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{run}(\texttt{rc : RobotController})} \\
\{ Menjalankan robot utama dan memanggil perilaku sesuai tipe robot. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{robotType} : RobotType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{turnCount} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}print ``I'm alive'' \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{rc.setIndicatorString("Hello world")} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}while robot is alive do \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{turnCount} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{turnCount} + 1 \\

\hspace*{3em}try \\

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{robotType} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getType()} \\

\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{robotType = SOLDIER} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{Soldier.runSoldier(rc)} \\

\hspace*{4.5em}else if \texttt{robotType = MOPPER} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{Mopper.runMopper(rc)} \\

\hspace*{4.5em}else if \texttt{robotType = SPLASHER} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{Splasher.runSplasher(rc)} \\

\hspace*{4.5em}else \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{Tower.runTower(rc)} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}catch GameActionException \\
\hspace*{4.5em}print ``GameActionException'' \\

\hspace*{3em}catch Exception \\
\hspace*{4.5em}print ``Exception'' \\

\hspace*{3em}finally \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{Clock.yield()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endwhile

\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Soldier.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runSoldier}(\texttt{rc : RobotController})} \\
\{ Mengatur perilaku soldier berdasarkan fase permainan. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{visible} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Comms.startTurn(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{captureSpawnTower(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Comms.enemyTowerLoc} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Comms.enemyTowerLoc} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{visible} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{findVisibleEnemyTower(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{visible} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{visible.getLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{Comms.reportEnemyTower(rc, knownEnemyTower)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getRoundNum()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{round} $\leq 200$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runEarly(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{round} $\leq 1000$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runMid(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runLate(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}

\textbf{procedure \texttt{runEarly}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Strategi eksplorasi awal dan penyerangan tower musuh. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{target} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{knownEnemyTower} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{target} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{getSymmetryTarget(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{target} $\neq$ null then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardLocation(rc, target)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{lastDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paintCurrentTileIfNeeded(rc)}

\end{pseudobox}
\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runMid}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase tengah permainan dengan fokus pada tower musuh. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardLocation(rc, knownEnemyTower)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{lastDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runEarly(rc)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paintCurrentTileIfNeeded(rc)}

\end{pseudobox}
\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runLate}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase akhir permainan dengan pembangunan tower dari ruin. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{curRuin} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{findNearbyRuin(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{curRuin} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{buildRuin(rc, curRuin.getMapLocation())} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardEnemyPaint(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{lastDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{enemy} in \texttt{rc.senseNearbyRobots(-1, rc.getTeam().opponent())} do \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{rc.canAttack(enemy.getLocation())} then \\

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.attack(enemy.getLocation())} \\

\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{isTowerType(enemy.getType())} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{Comms.reportEnemyTower(rc, enemy.getLocation())} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{4.5em}break \\

\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paintCurrentTileIfNeeded(rc)}

\vspace{0.5em}

\textbf{function \texttt{findNearbyRuin}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ MapInfo} \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{tile} in \texttt{rc.senseNearbyMapInfos()} do \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{tile.hasRuin()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return \texttt{tile} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return null

\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Splasher.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runSplasher}(\texttt{rc : RobotController})} \\
\{ Mengatur perilaku unit splasher berdasarkan fase permainan. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Comms.startTurn(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Comms.enemyTowerLoc} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Comms.enemyTowerLoc} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getRoundNum()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{round} $\leq 600$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runEarly(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{round} $\leq 1000$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runMid(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runLate(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\end{pseudobox}
\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runEarly}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase awal splasher bergerak menuju tower musuh atau pusat map. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardLocation(rc, knownEnemyTower)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardNearestEnemyTower(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} = null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardCenter(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{rc.canAttack(rc.getLocation())} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.attack(rc.getLocation())} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Soldier.paintCurrentTileIfNeeded(rc)}

\end{pseudobox}
\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runMid}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase tengah permainan splasher fokus mendekati tower musuh. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardLocation(rc, knownEnemyTower)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardNearestEnemyTower(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} = null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardCorner(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Soldier.tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Soldier.tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Soldier.paintCurrentTileIfNeeded(rc)}

\end{pseudobox}
\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runLate}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase akhir permainan dengan fokus menyerang area musuh. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{knownEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardLocation(rc, knownEnemyTower)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardNearestEnemyTower(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Soldier.tryAttackEnemyTower(rc)} then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} = null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardCorner(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{ahead} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getLocation().add(dir)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{ahead} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getLocation()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dirInfo} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseMapInfo(ahead)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dirInfo.getPaint()} = \texttt{ENEMY\_PRIMARY} \\
\hspace*{3em}or \texttt{dirInfo.getPaint()} = \texttt{ENEMY\_SECONDARY} \\
\hspace*{3em}or \texttt{dirInfo.getPaint()} = \texttt{EMPTY} then \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{rc.canAttack(rc.getLocation())} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.attack(rc.getLocation())} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Mopper.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runMopper}(\texttt{rc : RobotController})} \\
\{ Mengatur perilaku unit mopper berdasarkan fase permainan. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Comms.startTurn(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getRoundNum()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{round} $\leq 600$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{Soldier.runEarly(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{round} $\leq 1000$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runMid(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{runLate(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runMid}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase tengah permainan dengan fokus mengejar musuh terdekat. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemies} : RobotInfo array \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearestEnemy} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nextLoc} : MapLocation \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemies} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseNearbyRobots(-1, opponent team)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearestEnemy} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} $\leftarrow \infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{e} in \texttt{enemies} do \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ distanceSquared(\texttt{rc location}, \texttt{e location}) \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{dist} < \texttt{closestDist} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{closestDist} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dist} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{nearestEnemy} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{e location} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{nearestEnemy} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardLocation(rc, nearestEnemy)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardNearestAllySoldier(rc)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{dir} = null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardEnemyPaint(rc)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ null \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getLocation().add(dir)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMopSwing(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.mopSwing(dir)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{nextLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canAttack(nextLoc)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.attack(nextLoc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runLate}(\texttt{rc})} \\
\{ Fase akhir permainan dengan fokus membersihkan area musuh. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nextLoc} : MapLocation \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardNearestAllySoldier(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} = null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveTowardEnemyPaint(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ null \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getLocation().add(dir)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canMopSwing(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.mopSwing(dir)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{nextLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canAttack(nextLoc)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.attack(nextLoc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Tower.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runTower}(\texttt{rc : RobotController})} \\
\{ Mengatur perilaku tower dalam menyerang musuh dan memproduksi robot. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{mod} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nextType} : UnitType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{allowFallbackSoldier} : boolean \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{messages} : Message*array \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Comms.startTurn(rc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getRoundNum()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if there are nearby enemy robots then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tryAttackEnemies(rc)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{trySpawn(rc, MOPPER, false)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{round} $\leq 300$ then \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{buildCount mod 3 = 1} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{trySpawn(rc, SOLDIER, true)} \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{trySpawn(rc, SOLDIER, false)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{trySpawn(rc, SOLDIER, true)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{round} $\leq 1000$ then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{mod} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{buildCount mod 2} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{mod = 0} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{nextType} $\leftarrow$ SPLASHER \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{nextType} $\leftarrow$ SOLDIER \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{allowFallbackSoldier} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nextType} $\neq$ SPLASHER \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{trySpawn(rc, nextType, allowFallbackSoldier)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}else \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{mod} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{buildCount mod 2} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{mod = 0} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{nextType} $\leftarrow$ SPLASHER \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{nextType} $\leftarrow$ MOPPER \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{allowFallbackSoldier} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nextType} $\neq$ SPLASHER \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{trySpawn(rc, nextType, allowFallbackSoldier)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{messages} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.readMessages(all)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{m} in \texttt{messages} do \\
\hspace*{3em}print message sender and content \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{getBestSpawnLocation}(\texttt{rc, unitType}) $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
\{ Menentukan lokasi spawn terbaik berdasarkan posisi robot lain dan pusat map. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{center} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestLoc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{center} $\leftarrow$ middle of the map \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestLoc} $\leftarrow$ null \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{d} in \texttt{allDirections} do \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ rc location + d \\

\hspace*{3em}if robot cannot be built at \texttt{nextLoc} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 0$ \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ score $-$ distance(nextLoc, center) \\

\hspace*{3em}for each ally robot do \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ score + distance(nextLoc, ally location) \\
\hspace*{3em}endfor \\

\hspace*{3em}for each enemy robot do \\

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ score $-$ distance(nextLoc, enemy location) \\

\hspace*{4.5em}if enemy is a tower then \\
\hspace*{6em}report enemy tower location \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}endfor \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score} $>$ \texttt{bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ score \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestLoc} $\leftarrow$ nextLoc \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{bestLoc}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{trySpawn}(\texttt{rc, unitType, allowFallbackSoldier}) $\rightarrow$ boolean}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{getBestSpawnLocation(rc, unitType)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{nextLoc} = null and \texttt{allowFallbackSoldier} then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{getBestSpawnLocation(rc, SOLDIER)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{unitType} $\leftarrow$ SOLDIER \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{nextLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{rc.canBuildRobot(unitType, nextLoc)} then \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.buildRobot(unitType, nextLoc)} \\
\hspace*{3em}inform communication system about new spawn \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{buildCount} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{buildCount + 1} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{tryAttackEnemies}(\texttt{rc})}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each enemy robot nearby do \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{rc.canAttack(enemy location)} then \\

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.attack(enemy location)} \\

\hspace*{4.5em}if enemy is a tower then \\
\hspace*{6em}report enemy tower location \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.attack(null)} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return \\

\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
