\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Comms.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{encode}(\texttt{type, loc}) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Mengubah tipe pesan dan lokasi menjadi satu nilai integer. \}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return (type shifted left by 12) OR (loc.x shifted left by 6) OR loc.y
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{decodeType}(\texttt{bytes}) $\rightarrow$ integer}

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return (bytes shifted right by 12) AND 3
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{decodeLoc}(\texttt{bytes}) $\rightarrow$ MapLocation}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{x} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{y} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{x} $\leftarrow$ (bytes shifted right by 6) AND 63 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{y} $\leftarrow$ bytes AND 63 \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return MapLocation(x, y)
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{startTurn}(\texttt{rc : RobotController})} \\
\{ Membaca pesan dari robot lain dan memperbarui informasi tower musuh. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{messages} : Message array \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bytes} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{Motion.addtoQueue(current robot location)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{messages} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.readMessages(all)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{m} in \texttt{messages} do \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{bytes} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{m.getBytes()} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{decodeType(bytes)} = TYPE\_ENEMY\_TOWER then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{enemyTowerLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{decodeLoc(bytes)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{broadcast}(\texttt{rc, bytes})} \\
\{ Mengirim pesan ke semua robot sekutu di sekitar. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{allies} : RobotInfo array \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{allies} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseNearbyRobots(all, ally team)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for each \texttt{ally} in \texttt{allies} do \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{rc.canSendMessage(ally location, bytes)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{rc.sendMessage(ally location, bytes)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor
    
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{reportEnemyTower}(\texttt{rc, loc})} \\
\{ Melaporkan lokasi tower musuh ke robot sekutu. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{message} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{loc} = null then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemyTowerLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{loc} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{message} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{encode(TYPE\_ENEMY\_TOWER, loc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{broadcast(rc, message)}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{informNewSpawn}(\texttt{rc, spawnLoc})} \\
\{ Mengirim informasi tower musuh kepada robot yang baru dibuat. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{message} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{enemyTowerLoc} = null then return endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{message} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{encode(TYPE\_ENEMY\_TOWER, enemyTowerLoc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{rc.canSendMessage(spawnLoc, message)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{rc.sendMessage(spawnLoc, message)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Motion.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardLocation}(\texttt{rc, target}) $\rightarrow$ Direction} \\
\{ Menggerakkan robot menuju lokasi target menggunakan kombinasi greedy dan bug navigation. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{myLoc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{direct} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{curDist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{follow} : Direction \\

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{target} = null then return null endif

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{myLoc} $\leftarrow$ current robot location

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{myLoc = target} then return null endif

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bugTarget} $\neq$ \texttt{target} then reset bug navigation variables endif

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{direct} $\leftarrow$ direction from myLoc to target

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{curDist} $\leftarrow$ distance(myLoc, target)

\hspace*{1.5em}if currently tracing wall AND robot can move directly to target AND curDist $<$ bugStartDist then \\
\hspace*{3em}stop tracing wall
\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}if not tracing wall then

\hspace*{3em}if robot can move directly toward target then return direct endif

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{getBestDirection(rc, target)}

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{best} $\neq$ null then return best endif

\hspace*{3em}start wall tracing

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}if tracing steps exceed limit then reverse tracing side endif

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{follow} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{traceWall(rc)}

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{follow} $\neq$ null then return follow endif

\hspace*{1.5em}if robot can move directly to target then return direct endif

\hspace*{1.5em}return null
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{getBestDirection}(\texttt{rc, target}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ null

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$

\hspace*{1.5em}for each direction d do

\hspace*{3em}if robot cannot move in direction d then continue endif

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nextLoc} $\leftarrow$ location after moving to d

\hspace*{3em}if nextLoc exists in recent location queue then continue endif

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 0$

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ score $-$ distance(nextLoc, target) × 2

\hspace*{3em}for each ally robot do

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ score + distance(nextLoc, ally)

\hspace*{3em}endfor

\hspace*{3em}for each enemy robot do

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ score $-$ distance(nextLoc, enemy)

\hspace*{3em}endfor

\hspace*{3em}for each nearby tile do

\hspace*{4.5em}if tile is wall then score $\leftarrow$ score $-$ large penalty endif

\hspace*{3em}endfor

\hspace*{3em}if tile paint is ally color then decrease score endif

\hspace*{3em}if tile paint is enemy or empty then increase score endif

\hspace*{3em}if score $>$ bestScore then

\hspace*{4.5em}bestScore $\leftarrow$ score

\hspace*{4.5em}bestDir $\leftarrow$ d

\hspace*{3em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}return bestDir
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{traceWall}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ bugTraceDir

\hspace*{1.5em}if bugSide is right then

\hspace*{3em}rotate direction clockwise until movable

\hspace*{1.5em}else

\hspace*{3em}rotate direction counterclockwise until movable

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}if movable direction found then

\hspace*{3em}update bugTraceDir

\hspace*{3em}increase trace step count

\hspace*{3em}return direction

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}return null

\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardEnemyPaint}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ null

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} $\leftarrow \infty$

\hspace*{1.5em}for each tile in rc.senseNearbyMapInfos() do

\hspace*{3em}if tile paint is ENEMY\_PRIMARY OR ENEMY\_SECONDARY then

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ distance(robot location, tile location)

\hspace*{4.5em}if dist $<$ closestDist then

\hspace*{6em}closestDist $\leftarrow$ dist

\hspace*{6em}bestTarget $\leftarrow$ tile location

\hspace*{4.5em}endif

\hspace*{3em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}if bestTarget $\neq$ null then

\hspace*{3em}return moveTowardLocation(rc, bestTarget)

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}return moveTowardCorner(rc)
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardNearestEnemy}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemies} : RobotInfo array \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearest} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dist} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemies} $\leftarrow$ rc.senseNearbyRobots(opponent team)

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearest} $\leftarrow$ null

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} $\leftarrow \infty$

\hspace*{1.5em}for each enemy e in enemies do

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ distance between robot location and enemy location

\hspace*{3em}if dist $<$ closestDist then

\hspace*{4.5em}closestDist $\leftarrow$ dist

\hspace*{4.5em}nearest $\leftarrow$ enemy location

\hspace*{3em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}if nearest $\neq$ null then

\hspace*{3em}return moveTowardLocation(rc, nearest)

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}return moveTowardCenter(rc)
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardNearestEnemyTower}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemies} : RobotInfo array \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearest} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dist} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemies} $\leftarrow$ rc.senseNearbyRobots(opponent team)

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearest} $\leftarrow$ null

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} $\leftarrow \infty$

\hspace*{1.5em}for each enemy e in enemies do

\hspace*{3em}if isTowerType(e.type) then

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ distance between robot location and enemy location

\hspace*{4.5em}if dist $<$ closestDist then

\hspace*{6em}closestDist $\leftarrow$ dist

\hspace*{6em}nearest $\leftarrow$ enemy location

\hspace*{4.5em}endif

\hspace*{3em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}if nearest $\neq$ null then

\hspace*{3em}return moveTowardLocation(rc, nearest)

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}return moveTowardCorner(rc)
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardNearestAllySoldier}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{allies} : RobotInfo array \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearest} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dist} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{allies} $\leftarrow$ rc.senseNearbyRobots(ally team)

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearest} $\leftarrow$ null

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closestDist} $\leftarrow \infty$

\hspace*{1.5em}for each ally a in allies do

\hspace*{3em}if a.type = SOLDIER then

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ distance between robot location and ally location

\hspace*{4.5em}if dist $<$ closestDist then

\hspace*{6em}closestDist $\leftarrow$ dist

\hspace*{6em}nearest $\leftarrow$ ally location

\hspace*{4.5em}endif

\hspace*{3em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}if nearest $\neq$ null then

\hspace*{3em}return moveTowardLocation(rc, nearest)

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}return null
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardCenter}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{mapH} $\leftarrow$ rc.getMapHeight()

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{mapW} $\leftarrow$ rc.getMapWidth()

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{center} $\leftarrow$ MapLocation(mapW / 2, mapH / 2)

\hspace*{1.5em}return moveTowardLocation(rc, center)
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveTowardCorner}(\texttt{rc}) $\rightarrow$ Direction}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{corners} : list of MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{myLoc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{targetCorner} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{farthestDist} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dist} : integer \\

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{corners} $\leftarrow$ list of map corners and edge midpoints

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{myLoc} $\leftarrow$ robot location

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{targetCorner} $\leftarrow$ lastCornerTarget

\hspace*{1.5em}if targetCorner = null then

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{farthestDist} $\leftarrow -\infty$

\hspace*{3em}for each corner c in corners do

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ distance between myLoc and c

\hspace*{4.5em}if dist $>$ farthestDist then

\hspace*{6em}farthestDist $\leftarrow$ dist

\hspace*{6em}targetCorner $\leftarrow$ c

\hspace*{4.5em}endif

\hspace*{3em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}if distance between myLoc and targetCorner $\leq$ threshold then

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{idx} $\leftarrow$ index of targetCorner in corners

\hspace*{3em}if robot ID is even then

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{step} $\leftarrow 1$

\hspace*{3em}else

\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{step} $\leftarrow 3$

\hspace*{3em}endif

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{targetCorner} $\leftarrow$ corners[(idx + step) mod number of corners]

\hspace*{1.5em}endif

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{lastCornerTarget} $\leftarrow$ targetCorner

\hspace*{1.5em}return moveTowardLocation(rc, targetCorner)

\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{addtoQueue}(\texttt{loc})}

\textbf{Algorithm}

\hspace*{1.5em}for i from last index of queue down to 1 do

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{queueLastLocations[i]} $\leftarrow$ queueLastLocations[i - 1]

\hspace*{1.5em}endfor

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{queueLastLocations[0]} $\leftarrow$ loc
\end{pseudobox}

\subsubsection{Algoritma Solusi Greedy 3}

\begin{enumerate}[label=\alph*.]

\item \texttt{RobotPlayer.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{run}(input rc : RobotController)} \\
\{ Menjalankan bot utama secara terus-menerus. \\
Input: \texttt{rc}, controller unit yang sedang aktif
\}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{G.init(rc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}while true do \\
\hspace*{3em}try \\
\hspace*{4.5em}call \texttt{G.update()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}case \texttt{rc.getType()} of \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{SOLDIER}  : call \texttt{Robot.runSoldier()} \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{MOPPER}   : call \texttt{Robot.runMopper()} \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{SPLASHER} : call \texttt{Robot.runSplasher()} \\
\hspace*{6em}else             : call \texttt{Tower.run()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endcase \\
\hspace*{3em}catch exception \texttt{e} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}print ``Err:'' + exception message \\
\hspace*{3em}endtry \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Clock.yield()}  \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endwhile
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{G.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{init}(input controller : RobotController)} \\
\{ Inisialisasi seluruh variabel global yang dipakai bot. \\
Input: \texttt controller robot atau tower yang aktif
\}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{initAllies} : array of RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{rc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{controller} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{team} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getTeam()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{opponent} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{team.opponent()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{me} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getLocation()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{myType} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getType()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{isRobotUnit} $\leftarrow$ (\texttt{myType = SOLDIER}) or (\texttt{myType = MOPPER}) or (\texttt{myType = SPLASHER}) \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{moneyPattern} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getTowerPattern(LEVEL\_ONE\_MONEY\_TOWER)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paintPattern} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getTowerPattern(LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{defensePattern} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getTowerPattern(LEVEL\_ONE\_DEFENSE\_TOWER)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{recentLocs[0]} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{me} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{histPtr} $\leftarrow 1$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{lastHistRound} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getRoundNum()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{isRobotUnit} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{initAllies} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseNearbyRobots(-1, team)} \\
\hspace*{3em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ panjang(\texttt{initAllies})-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{Util.isTower(initAllies[i].type)} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{spawnBase} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{initAllies[i].location} \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{lastSeenAllyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{spawnBase} \\
\hspace*{6em}break  \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{spawnBase} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{me} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{lastSeenAllyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{me} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{spawnBase} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{inferredEnemyBase} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Util.inferEnemyBase(spawnBase)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{inferredEnemyBase} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{pushTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{inferredEnemyBase} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{pushTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Util.pickFrontierLikeTarget()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{pushTurnsLeft} $\leftarrow 60$
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{update}()} \\
\{ Memperbarui state global setiap ronde. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cur} : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cur} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getLocation()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{cur} $\neq$ \texttt{me} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{prevPrevLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{prevLoc} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{prevLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{me} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{me} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{cur} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{round} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getRoundNum()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{chips} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.getChips()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearbyAllies} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseNearbyRobots(-1, team)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearbyEnemies} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseNearbyRobots(-1, opponent)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearbyTiles} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{rc.senseNearbyMapInfos()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearbyAllyLen} $\leftarrow$ panjang(\texttt{nearbyAllies}) \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearbyEnemyLen} $\leftarrow$ panjang(\texttt{nearbyEnemies}) \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nearbyTileLen} $\leftarrow$ panjang(\texttt{nearbyTiles}) \\

\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{rememberTowers()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{updatePushTarget()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{round} $\neq$ \texttt{lastHistRound} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{recentLocs[histPtr mod HIST]} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{me} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{histPtr} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{histPtr} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{lastHistRound} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{round} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{rememberTowers}()} \\
\{ Mengingat lokasi tower sekutu dan musuh yang terlihat. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{r} : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nearbyAllyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{r} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nearbyAllies[i]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{Util.isTower(r.type)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{lastSeenAllyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{r.location} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{r} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nearbyEnemies[i]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{Util.isTower(r.type)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{lastSeenEnemyTower} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{r.location} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{inferredEnemyBase} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{r.location} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{updatePushTarget}()} \\
\{ Menentukan target dorongan berikutnya. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{needNew} : boolean

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{needNew} $\leftarrow$ (\texttt{pushTarget = null}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{pushTurnsLeft} $\leq 0$) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (jarakKuadrat(\texttt{me, pushTarget}) $\leq 9$) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{prevLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{prevPrevLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{me = prevPrevLoc}) \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{needNew} then \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{lastSeenEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{pushTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{lastSeenEnemyTower} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{pushTurnsLeft} $\leftarrow 30$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{inferredEnemyBase} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{pushTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{inferredEnemyBase} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{pushTurnsLeft} $\leftarrow 40$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{pushTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Util.pickFrontierLikeTarget()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{pushTurnsLeft} $\leftarrow 22$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{pushTurnsLeft} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{pushTurnsLeft} - 1 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{timesVisited}(input loc : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Menghitung berapa kali sebuah lokasi muncul di riwayat kunjungan. \\
Input: \texttt{loc}, lokasi yang ingin dicek
\}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cnt, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cnt} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{HIST}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{recentLocs[i]} $\neq$ null and \texttt{recentLocs[i] = loc} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{cnt} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{cnt} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{cnt}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{paintPct}() $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Mengembalikan persentase paint unit saat ini. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cap} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cap} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{myType.paintCapacity} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{cap} $\leq 0$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return 100 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{paint * 100 div cap} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Util.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{isTower}(input type : UnitType) $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Mengecek apakah sebuah tipe unit adalah tower. \\
Input: tipe unit
\}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_ONE\_PAINT\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_TWO\_PAINT\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_THREE\_PAINT\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_ONE\_MONEY\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_TWO\_MONEY\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_THREE\_MONEY\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_ONE\_DEFENSE\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_TWO\_DEFENSE\_TOWER}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}or (\texttt{type = LEVEL\_THREE\_DEFENSE\_TOWER})
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{inferEnemyBase}(input allyBase : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
\{ Menginfer posisi base musuh dengan asumsi simetri rotasi peta. \\
Input:  lokasi base sekutu
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{w, h} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{w} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getMapWidth()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{h} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getMapHeight()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{MapLocation(w - 1 - allyBase.x, h - 1 - allyBase.y)}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{randomMapLocation}() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
\{ Menghasilkan lokasi acak di dalam peta. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{MapLocation(} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rng.nextInt(G.rc.getMapWidth()),} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rng.nextInt(G.rc.getMapHeight())} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{)}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{pickFrontierLikeTarget}() $\rightarrow$ MapLocation} \\
\{ Memilih target acak yang cenderung menuju frontier atau area kontestasi. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{w, h} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best, cand} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{w} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getMapWidth()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{h} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getMapHeight()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow 9$ downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{cand} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{MapLocation(G.rng.nextInt(w), G.rng.nextInt(h))} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 0$ \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.lastSeenEnemyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{cand, G.lastSeenEnemyTower}) div 3 \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + jarakKuadrat(\texttt{cand, G.me}) div 4 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.lastSeenAllyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + jarakKuadrat(\texttt{cand, G.lastSeenAllyTower}) div 8 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{cand} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{best} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{best} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{randomMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Logistics.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{needRefillSoldier}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Menentukan apakah Soldier perlu refill paint. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint} $\leq 35$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint} $\leq 60$ and \texttt{farFromSupply()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint} $\leq 80$ and \texttt{G.lastSeenAllyTower} $\neq$ null \\
\hspace*{3em}and jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.lastSeenAllyTower}) $\leq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{needRefillMopper}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Menentukan apakah Mopper perlu refill paint. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return (\texttt{G.paint} $\leq 20$) or (\texttt{G.paint} $\leq 35$ and \texttt{farFromSupply()})
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{needRefillSplasher}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Menentukan apakah Splasher perlu refill paint. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return (\texttt{G.paint} $\leq 80$) or (\texttt{G.paint} $\leq 120$ and \texttt{farFromSupply()})
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{farFromSupply}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Mengecek apakah unit jauh dari sumber paint sekutu terdekat. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{G.lastSeenAllyTower} $\neq$ null \\
\hspace*{4.5em}and jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.lastSeenAllyTower}) > 30
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{goRefill}()} \\
\{ Mencoba refill paint atau bergerak menuju tower sekutu. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{tryWithdrawAdjacent()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return  \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.lastSeenAllyTower} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Motion.moveToward(G.lastSeenAllyTower, false)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Motion.safeFallbackMove(false)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryWithdrawAdjacent}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Mengambil paint dari tower sekutu yang cukup dekat. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cap, want, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{ally} : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if not \texttt{G.isRobotUnit} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cap} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.myType.paintCapacity} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint} $\geq 9 * cap \div 10$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{ally} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllies[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{Util.isTower(ally.type)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, ally.location}) > 2 then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{want} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{cap - G.paint} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.rc.canTransferPaint(ally.location, -want)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{G.rc.transferPaint(ally.location, -want)} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{G.paint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return true \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryTransferPaintToAlly}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Mopper menyalurkan paint ke ally yang kekurangan paint. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestAlly} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, i, pct, score, amount} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{at} : UnitType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{ally} : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.myType} $\neq$ \texttt{MOPPER} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint} $\leq 30$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestAlly} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{ally} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllies[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{ally.location = G.me} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{at} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{ally.type} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{at} $\neq$ \texttt{SOLDIER} and \texttt{at} $\neq$ \texttt{SPLASHER} and \texttt{at} $\neq$ \texttt{MOPPER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{pct} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{ally.paintAmount * 100 div at.paintCapacity} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{pct} > 55 then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{pct} $\leq 10$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 80$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{pct} $\leq 25$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 50$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{pct} $\leq 40$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 25$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 10$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{at = SOLDIER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 15 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{at = SPLASHER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 10 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 2 * jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, ally.location}) \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestAlly} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{ally} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestAlly = null} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{amount} $\leftarrow$ minimum(minimum(\texttt{G.paint - 30, bestAlly.type.paintCapacity - bestAlly.paintAmount}), 70) \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{amount} $\leq 5$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, bestAlly.location}) $\leq 2$ \\
\hspace*{3em}and \texttt{G.rc.canTransferPaint(bestAlly.location, amount)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.transferPaint(bestAlly.location, amount)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ arah dari \texttt{G.me} ke \texttt{bestAlly.location} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir} $\neq$ \texttt{CENTER} and \texttt{G.rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Motion.executeMove(dir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Motion.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{executeMove}(input dir : Direction)} \\
\{ Melakukan gerakan satu langkah jika valid, lalu memperbarui histori posisi. \\
Input: \texttt{dir}, arah gerak
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{dir = null} or \texttt{dir = CENTER} or not \texttt{G.rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{G.rc.move(dir)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{G.lastMoveDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{G.prevPrevLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.prevLoc} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{G.prevLoc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.me} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{G.me} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getLocation()}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{moveToward}(input target : MapLocation, input aggressive : boolean) $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Bergerak ke arah target dengan memilih arah ber-skor terbaik. \\
Input: \texttt{target}, target gerak; \texttt{aggressive}, mode gerak
\}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir, dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, curDist, nxtDist, score, di} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nxt} : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{target = null} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{curDist} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, target}) \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{di} $\leftarrow 7$ downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.DIRS[di]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nxt} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.me + dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nxtDist} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, target}) \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Greedy.scoreMove(nxt, aggressive)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{nxtDist < curDist} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{aggressive} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 42 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}else \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 32 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{nxtDist > curDist} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{aggressive} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 32 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}else \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 24 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestDir} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{executeMove(bestDir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{followWeakAlly}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Mencari ally yang paint-nya rendah lalu mendekatinya. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best, ally} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, i, pct, score} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{at} : UnitType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{ally} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllies[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{ally.location = G.me} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{at} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{ally.type} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{at} $\neq$ \texttt{SOLDIER} and \texttt{at} $\neq$ \texttt{SPLASHER} and \texttt{at} $\neq$ \texttt{MOPPER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{pct} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{ally.paintAmount * 100 div at.paintCapacity} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{pct} > 40 then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{pct} $\leq 10$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 50$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{pct} $\leq 25$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 30$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 15$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{at = SOLDIER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 8 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, ally.location}) \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{ally} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{best} $\neq$ null and \texttt{moveToward(best.location, false)}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{safeFallbackMove}(input aggressive : boolean)} \\
\{ Gerakan fallback jika tidak ada gerakan utama yang dipilih. \\
Input: \texttt{aggressive}, mode scoring gerak
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir, dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, d, di} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nxt} : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{di} $\leftarrow 7$ downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.DIRS[di]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nxt} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.me + dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Greedy.scoreMove(nxt, aggressive)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.pushTarget} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.pushTarget}) - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, G.pushTarget}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{d > 0} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 16 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}else if \texttt{d < 0} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 12 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestDir} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{executeMove(bestDir)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Greedy.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryAttackEnemyTower}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Menyerang tower musuh terbaik yang ada dalam jangkauan. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best, e} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{e} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{Util.isTower(e.type)} or not \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(e.location)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 120$ \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{e.health} $\leq 100$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 60 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, e.location}) \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{e} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{best} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.attack(best.location)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.paint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paint} - 5 \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryAttackEnemyRobotEfficient}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Menyerang robot musuh non-tower bila tile target bukan tile ally. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{e} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tileInfo} : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tp} : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{e} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{Util.isTower(e.type)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(e.location)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tileInfo} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{findCachedTileOrSense(e.location)} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{tileInfo = null} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tp} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tileInfo.getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{tp.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.attack(e.location, false)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.paint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paint} - 5 \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryBestPaintAttack}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Memilih tile terbaik untuk diwarnai oleh Soldier. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestSec} : boolean \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, s1, s2, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tile} : MapInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestSec} $\leftarrow$ false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tile} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(tile.getMapLocation())} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{s1} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{scorePaintTarget(tile, false)} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{s1 > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{s1} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tile.getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestSec} $\leftarrow$ false \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{s2} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{scorePaintTarget(tile, true)} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{s2 > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{s2} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tile.getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestSec} $\leftarrow$ true \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestTarget} $\neq$ null and \texttt{bestScore} > 5 then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.attack(bestTarget, bestSec)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.paint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paint} - 5 \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryCleanEnemyPaint}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Mopper membersihkan cat musuh terbaik atau melakukan mop swing terbaik. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget, loc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, bestSwingScore, i, j, di} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tile} : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestSwing, dir} : Direction

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tile} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i]} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tile.getPaint()} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p = EMPTY} or \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{loc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tile.getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(loc)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 30$ - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, loc}) + \texttt{lightFrontierBonus(loc)} \\

\hspace*{3em}for \texttt{j} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[j].location = loc} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 25 \\
\hspace*{6em}break \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endfor \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{loc} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestTarget} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.attack(bestTarget)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestSwing} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestSwingScore} $\leftarrow 5$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{di} $\leftarrow 3$ downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.DIRS[2*di]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canMopSwing(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{estimateSwingScore(dir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestSwingScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestSwingScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestSwing} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestSwing} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.mopSwing(bestSwing)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryBestSplashAttack}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Memilih pusat splash attack terbaik untuk Splasher. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget, loc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint < 55} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return false \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{loc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(loc)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{splashValue(loc)} - 35 \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestTarget} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{loc} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestTarget} $\neq$ null and \texttt{bestScore} > 0 then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.attack(bestTarget)} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.paint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paint} - 50 \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryExpandMove}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Memilih gerakan ekspansi terbaik untuk Soldier. \} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir, dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, d, di} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nxt} : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{di} $\leftarrow 7$ downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.DIRS[di]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nxt} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.me + dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{scoreMove(nxt, false)} \\
\hspace*{6em}+ 5 * \texttt{frontierBonusLight(nxt)} \\
\hspace*{6em}+ 2 * \texttt{localPaintOpportunity(nxt)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.pushTarget} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.pushTarget}) - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, G.pushTarget}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{d > 0} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 30 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}else if \texttt{d < 0} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 25 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestDir} $\neq$ null and \texttt{bestScore} > 0 then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Motion.executeMove(bestDir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryContestMove}() $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
\{ Memilih gerakan kontestasi terbaik untuk Splasher. \}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir, dir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, d, di} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nxt} : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{di} $\leftarrow 7$ downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.DIRS[di]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canMove(dir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{nxt} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.me + dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{scoreMove(nxt, true)} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.pushTarget} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.pushTarget}) - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, G.pushTarget}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{d > 0} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 26 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}else if \texttt{d < 0} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 20 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestDir} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{dir} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{bestDir} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Motion.executeMove(bestDir)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return true \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return false
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{scoreMove}(input nxt : MapLocation, input aggressive : boolean) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Memberi skor pada lokasi gerak kandidat. \\
Input: \texttt{nxt}, lokasi kandidat; \texttt{aggressive}, mode penilaian
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nxtInfo} : MapInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p, tp} : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paintPct, score, frontier, adjacent, drain, futurePaint, revisit} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{candDir} : Direction \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{d, dist, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{a} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tl} : MapLocation

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{nxtInfo} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{findCachedTileOrSense(nxt)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{nxtInfo} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{nxtInfo.getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{EMPTY} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{paintPct} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paintPct()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{paintPct} $\leq 15$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 30$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 5$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow -40$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{paintPct} $\leq 40$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 15$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 18$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow -20$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow -8$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 20$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{aggressive} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 12$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 6$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{frontier} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{adjacent} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{drain} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{drain} $\leftarrow 1$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{drain} $\leftarrow 2$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{tl} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{dist} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, tl}) \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{dist > 0} and \texttt{dist} $\leq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{tp} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{tp = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{frontier} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{frontier} + 2 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}else if not \texttt{tp.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{frontier} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{frontier} + 3 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{a} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllies[i]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{a.location} $\neq$ \texttt{G.me} and jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, a.location}) $\leq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{adjacent} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{adjacent} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 3 * \texttt{frontier} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 6 * \texttt{adjacent} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{futurePaint} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paint - drain - adjacent} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{futurePaint} $\leq 5$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 90 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{futurePaint} $\leq 12$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 40 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{futurePaint} $\leq 20$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 15 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{revisit} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.timesVisited(nxt)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 55 * \texttt{revisit} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.prevLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{nxt = G.prevLoc} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 100 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.prevPrevLoc} $\neq$ null and \texttt{nxt = G.prevPrevLoc} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 35 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{candDir} $\leftarrow$ arah dari \texttt{G.me} ke \texttt{nxt} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.lastMoveDir} $\neq$ \texttt{CENTER} then \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{candDir = opposite(G.lastMoveDir)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 60 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{candDir = G.lastMoveDir} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 10 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.lastSeenAllyTower} $\neq$ null and \texttt{paintPct} $\leq 40$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, G.lastSeenAllyTower}) div 3 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{nxt, G.nearbyEnemies[i].location}) \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{d} $\leq 4$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 6 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{d} $\leq 9$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 2 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{score}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{scorePaintTarget}(input tile : MapInfo, input useSec : boolean) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Memberi skor pada sebuah tile untuk aksi pewarnaan Soldier. \\
Input: \texttt{tile}, tile target; \texttt{useSec}, pilihan warna
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p, ap} : PaintType \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{loc, al} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score, allyN, enemyN, d, after, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tile.getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{loc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{tile.getMapLocation()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return -999 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 22$ \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.round} $\leq 140$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 15 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 30$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{allyN} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{enemyN} $\leftarrow 0$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{al} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{loc, al}) \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{d = 0} or \texttt{d > 2} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{ap} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{ap.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{allyN} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{allyN} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{ap} $\neq$ \texttt{EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{enemyN} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{enemyN} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{allyN} $\geq 2$ then \texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 8 endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{allyN} $\geq 4$ then \texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 12 endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{allyN} $\geq 6$ then \texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 18 endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 3 * \texttt{enemyN} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 2 * \texttt{lightFrontierBonus(loc)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].hasRuin()} and jarakKuadrat(\texttt{loc, G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()}) $\leq 8$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 15 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}break \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.inferredEnemyBase} $\neq$ null then \\
\hspace*{3em}if jarakKuadrat(\texttt{loc, G.inferredEnemyBase}) < jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.inferredEnemyBase}) then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 6 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{after} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.paint - 5} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{after} $\leq 0$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return -999 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{after} $\leq 10$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 80 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else if \texttt{after} $\leq 20$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 30 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, loc}) div 2 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{score}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{lightFrontierBonus}(input loc : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Menghitung bonus frontier ringan di sekitar satu tile. \\
Input: \texttt{loc}, lokasi yang dinilai
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bonus, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{adj} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} : PaintType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bonus} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{adj} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if jarakKuadrat(\texttt{loc, adj}) $\neq 1$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bonus} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{bonus} + 3 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if not \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bonus} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{bonus} + 5 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{bonus}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{frontierBonusLight}(input loc : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Bonus frontier untuk lokasi gerak dalam radius 1 atau 2. \\
Input: \texttt{loc}, lokasi yang dinilai
\}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bonus, d, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{adj} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} : PaintType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bonus} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{adj} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{loc, adj}) \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{d} $\neq 1$ and \texttt{d} $\neq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bonus} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{bonus} + 3 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if not \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bonus} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{bonus} + 5 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{bonus}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{localPaintOpportunity}(input center : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Menghitung potensi pewarnaan di sekitar satu lokasi. \\
Input: \texttt{center}, pusat evaluasi
\}\\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{v, d, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{adj} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} : PaintType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{v} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{adj} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{center, adj}) \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{d} $\neq 1$ and \texttt{d} $\neq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{v} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{v} + 2 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if not \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{v} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{v} + 3 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{v}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{countAdjacentAllies}(input loc : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Menghitung jumlah ally di sekitar satu lokasi. \\
Input: \texttt{loc}, lokasi yang dicek
\} \\

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cnt, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{a} : RobotInfo

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{cnt} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{a} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyAllies[i]} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{a.location} $\neq$ \texttt{G.me} and jarakKuadrat(\texttt{loc, a.location}) $\leq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{cnt} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{cnt} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{cnt}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{estimateSwingScore}(input dir : Direction) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Menghitung nilai mop swing ke satu arah. \\
Input: \texttt{dir}, arah swing
\}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{step1, step2, loc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score, d1, d2, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} : PaintType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{step1} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.me + dir} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{step2} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{step1 + dir} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 0$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{loc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if jarakKuadrat(\texttt{step1, loc}) $\leq 2$ or jarakKuadrat(\texttt{step2, loc}) $\leq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{p} $\neq$ \texttt{EMPTY} and not \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 10 \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{d1} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[i].location, step1}) \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{d2} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[i].location, step2}) \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{d1} $\leq 2$ or \texttt{d2} $\leq 2$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 15 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.paint} $\leq 20$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 15 \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{score}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{splashValue}(input center : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ integer} \\
\{ Menghitung nilai serangan splash untuk satu pusat serangan. \\
Input: \texttt{center}, pusat splash
\}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score, i} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{loc} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{p} : PaintType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{loc} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if jarakKuadrat(\texttt{center, loc}) > 4 then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{p} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{p = EMPTY} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 4 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if not \texttt{p.isAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 8 \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - 2 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\
\hspace*{1.5em}return \texttt{score}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{findCachedTileOrSense}(input loc : MapLocation) $\rightarrow$ MapInfo} \\
\{ Mencari info tile dari cache nearby, atau sense langsung jika bisa. \\
Input: \texttt{loc}, lokasi yang ingin dicek
\}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyTileLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i].getMapLocation() = loc} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return \texttt{G.nearbyTiles[i]} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.canSenseLocation(loc)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{G.rc.senseMapInfo(loc)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}return null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Robot.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runSoldier}()} \\
\{ Menjalankan perilaku Soldier setiap ronde. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{moved} : boolean

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{Logistics.tryWithdrawAdjacent()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{TowerBuilder.tryTowerRoutine()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Logistics.needRefillSoldier()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Logistics.goRefill()} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} and \texttt{Greedy.tryAttackEnemyTower()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Greedy.tryExpandMove()} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{moved} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Greedy.tryExpandMove()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if not \texttt{moved} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{moved} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveToward(G.pushTarget, true)} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{moved} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}call \texttt{Motion.safeFallbackMove(true)} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Logistics.tryWithdrawAdjacent()} \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{Greedy.tryAttackEnemyRobotEfficient()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if not \texttt{Greedy.tryAttackEnemyTower()} then \\
\hspace*{6em}if not \texttt{TowerBuilder.tryTowerRoutine()} then \\
\hspace*{7.5em}call \texttt{Greedy.tryBestPaintAttack()} \\
\hspace*{6em}endif \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runMopper}()} \\
\{ Menjalankan perilaku Mopper setiap ronde. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{closest} : MapLocation \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best, d, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Logistics.tryTransferPaintToAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Logistics.needRefillMopper()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Logistics.goRefill()} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} and \texttt{Greedy.tryCleanEnemyPaint()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Motion.followWeakAlly()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen > 0} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{closest} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow +\infty$ \\

\hspace*{3em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{d} $\leftarrow$ jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, G.nearbyEnemies[i].location}) \\
\hspace*{4.5em}if \texttt{d < best} then \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{d} \\
\hspace*{6em}\texttt{closest} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[i].location} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}endif \\
\hspace*{3em}endfor \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{closest} $\neq$ null and \texttt{Motion.moveToward(closest, false)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Motion.moveToward(G.pushTarget, false)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{Motion.safeFallbackMove(false)}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{runSplasher}()} \\
\{ Menjalankan perilaku Splasher setiap ronde. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{moved} : boolean

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{Logistics.tryWithdrawAdjacent()} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{Logistics.needRefillSplasher()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Logistics.goRefill()} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} and \texttt{Greedy.tryAttackEnemyTower()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} and \texttt{Greedy.tryBestSplashAttack()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{moved} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Greedy.tryContestMove()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if not \texttt{moved} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{moved} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{Motion.moveToward(G.pushTarget, false)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if not \texttt{moved} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Motion.safeFallbackMove(false)} \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}call \texttt{Logistics.tryWithdrawAdjacent()} \\
\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{Greedy.tryAttackEnemyTower()} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}call \texttt{Greedy.tryBestSplashAttack()} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\item \texttt{Tower.java}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{run}()} \\
\{ Menjalankan perilaku tower. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}-

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{tryAttack()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}call \texttt{trySpawn()}
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{tryAttack}()} \\
\{ Menyerang musuh dengan skor tertinggi yang ada dalam jangkauan tower. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best, e} : RobotInfo \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore, score, i} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ null \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow -\infty$ \\

\hspace*{1.5em}for \texttt{i} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemyLen}-1 downto 0 do \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{e} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.nearbyEnemies[i]} \\

\hspace*{3em}if not \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(e.location)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}continue \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{e.health} $\leq 50$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 80$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{e.health} $\leq 150$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 40$ \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow 10$ \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{Util.isTower(e.type)} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 50 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{e.type = SOLDIER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 25 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{e.type = SPLASHER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 20 \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} + 10 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\

\hspace*{3em}\texttt{score} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} - jarakKuadrat(\texttt{G.me, e.location}) \\

\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{score > bestScore} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{bestScore} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{score} \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{best} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{e} \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endfor \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{best} $\neq$ null and \texttt{G.rc.canAttack(best.location)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{G.rc.attack(best.location)} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{procedure \texttt{trySpawn}()} \\
\{ Menentukan apakah tower akan spawn unit lalu menambah counter spawn. \}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tp} : integer \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{toBuild} : UnitType

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if not \texttt{G.rc.isActionReady()} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{tp} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{G.rc.getPaint()} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{toBuild} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{chooseUnit(tp)} \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{toBuild = null} then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{tryBuild(toBuild)} then \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{toBuild = SOLDIER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{spawnedSoldiers} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{spawnedSoldiers} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{toBuild = MOPPER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{spawnedMoppers} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{spawnedMoppers} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}else if \texttt{toBuild = SPLASHER} then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}\texttt{spawnedSplashers} $\leftarrow$ \texttt{spawnedSplashers} + 1 \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{chooseUnit}(input tp : integer) $\rightarrow$ UnitType} \\
\{ Memilih jenis unit yang paling layak dibangun. \\
Input: \texttt{tp}, jumlah paint tower saat ini
\}

\textbf{Kamus:} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{wantMoppers, wantSplashers} : integer

\textbf{Algorithm} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{G.round} $\leq 40$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}if \texttt{tp} $\geq 200$ and \texttt{G.chips} $\geq 250$ then \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return \texttt{SOLDIER} \\
\hspace*{3em}else \\
\hspace*{4.5em}return null \\
\hspace*{3em}endif \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}\texttt{wantMoppers} $\leftarrow$ maksimum(1, \texttt{spawnedSoldiers div 3}) \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{spawnedSoldiers} $\geq 3$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{wantSplashers} $\leftarrow$ maksimum(1, \texttt{spawnedSoldiers div 5}) \\
\hspace*{1.5em}else \\
\hspace*{3em}\texttt{wantSplashers} $\leftarrow 0$ \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{spawnedMoppers < wantMoppers} and \texttt{tp} $\geq 100$ and \texttt{G.chips} $\geq 300$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{MOPPER} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{spawnedSplashers < wantSplashers} and \texttt{tp} $\geq 300$ and \texttt{G.chips} $\geq 400$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{SPLASHER} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}if \texttt{tp} $\geq 200$ and \texttt{G.chips} $\geq 250$ then \\
\hspace*{3em}return \texttt{SOLDIER} \\
\hspace*{1.5em}endif \\

\hspace*{1.5em}return null
\end{pseudobox}

\begin{pseudobox}
\textbf{function \texttt{tryBuild}(input type : UnitType) $\rightarrow$ boolean} \\
